We show that a symmetry fixes what those variables are: a network layer is a sum over interchangeable units, so relabeling the units leaves it unchanged; given smoothness and the condition that a unit's gradient vanish at the origin, symmetry then enforces a universal leading form for the expansion about the near-zero weights present at the start of training, the quadratic $\Tr[WW^{\top}A(x)]$, in which every architectural detail is confined to a single ``structure matrix" $A(x)$ that we compute for each architecture. Perceptrons, attention layers, mixtures of experts, and convolutions become one model at different $A$. Its training dynamics then close on the ``order parameter" $M=WW^{\top}$ and, whenever the data matrices share an eigenbasis, reduce to a Lotka--Volterra equation whose modes switch on one after another. The smaller the initial weights, the further apart the switch-on times, and the plateaus appear as a singular limit of a smooth flow; when many modes are unresolved the same events merge into a power law in training time whose exponent the theory predicts. We confirm both numerically across training methods and architectures.

</details>


### [167] [Where You Measure Decides What You Measure: Position Selection in Ablation-Based SAE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13337)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）的因果评估存在一个被忽视的协议问题：在测量latent被"关闭"的效果时，默认在最大激活token处测量，而这个位置由字典决定而非实验者决定。不同字典会在不同token处测量，导致词典差异被误读为latent差异。作者训练六种仅拟合设置不同的SAE，将7.6%和11.9%的方差归因于位置而非语义，修正后近乎为零；更大语料反而加剧该问题。作者提出一行代码的评估协议修正，并审计了五篇已发表论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器用于识别语言模型计算的概念，其评估标准做法是关闭某个latent并观察效果。然而，latent在多个token上激活，测量必须在一个token上进行，通常是在最大激活处。这个token选择几乎从不被报告，且由词典决定而非实验者决定。不同词典会导致测量位置不同，这使得跨SAE的因果比较变得不可靠，甚至可能完全失真。

Method: 作者通过三组实验验证该问题：(1) 对比Google发布的两个稀疏自编码器，通过解码器相似度匹配latent，发现即使编码几乎相同的latent对，两者也常在非常不同的token处测量；(2) 从同一初始化训练六个仅拟合设置不同的SAE，分离「词典分歧」和「测量位置分歧」的贡献，发现7.6%~11.9%的方差来自位置；(3) 在十六倍范围的语料规模下测试，发现语料越大，字典对测量位置的共识反而越差。最后提出一行代码的评估协议修正，并提供因果数值报告的标准，审计五篇已发表论文。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 即使两个词典几乎以相同方式编码同一latent，它们也会在相当大比例的latent上选择不同token进行测量；(2) 在受控条件下（同一初始化、仅拟合设置不同），比较结果中7.6%和11.9%的方差源于测量位置而非latent语义，修正后降至接近零；(3) 增加评估数据无法解决该问题，语料规模扩大十六倍后位置分歧反而增大；(4) 对五篇已发表论文的审计显示它们均未报告或控制测量位置。

Conclusion: 没有报告测量位置的因果数值，描述的不只是latent本身，还包含了它所测量的token。SAE的可比较评估必须规定统一的测量位置，这是一个简单可以修正但对结论影响重大的协议缺陷。作者提供的修正方案就是一行代码，使其易于被领域采纳。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are meant to name the things a language model computes, and the usual way to check that a latent matters is to switch it off and see what changes. But a latent fires at many tokens, and the effect has to be measured at one of them. The convention is to measure where the latent fires hardest. That choice is almost never reported, and it is not made by the experimenter: it is made by the dictionary under evaluation. Change the dictionary and the measurement moves to a different token. We show this is not a detail. Take two sparse autoencoders released by Google for the same model and match their latents by decoder similarity: even among the pairs the two dictionaries encode almost identically, they pick different tokens for a large share of them. Two dictionaries compared under the usual protocol are therefore very often compared at different places. To separate the convention from the dictionaries we train six autoencoders from one initialisation, differing only in fitting choices, so that a latent means the same thing in each. Most of the variance such a comparison reads as "these dictionaries disagree about this latent" turns out to be the position instead: it falls from 7.6% and 11.9% of variance to near zero once every dictionary is measured at the same token. More evaluation data does not rescue it. Across a sixteenfold range of corpus sizes the dictionaries agree less about where to measure, not more, so the problem grows with scale. The correction is one line of evaluation code. We give the protocol an ablation-based causal number must report to be comparable across papers, and an audit of five published papers against it. In short: a causal number reported without its position describes the token it was taken at as much as the latent it was taken from.

</details>


### [168] [Simulation-to-real transfer learning for infrared spectroscopic chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13341)
*Yusen Tan,Yixuan Chen,Zheng Fang,Pan Liu,Yifan Li,Qinyu Guo,Zhedong Lin,Yuqiang Li,Xiangxiang Zeng,Tong Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了UltraIR，一个参数量超过1亿的红外光谱基础模型，通过在约6000万个模拟红外光谱上进行预训练，实现从模拟到真实的迁移学习，在多种化学感知与分析任务中显著优于传统机器学习及任务特定的深度学习基线，且仅需少量标注实验光谱即可实现强性能，具备零样本跨仪器/跨实验室泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统红外光谱解读依赖人工经验、先验知识和参考光谱，劳动密集且难以规模化；现有机器学习方法多针对单一任务或数据集定制，需要大量标注训练数据，且在不同分析目标与实验数据集之间迁移能力差，缺乏通用的、数据高效的化学感知方案。

Method: UltraIR采用基础模型范式，包含超过1亿参数。预训练阶段在约6000万个模拟红外光谱上，通过三种自监督/辅助任务实现：光谱重构、分子指纹相似度对齐和官能团预测。预训练后通过任务特定的标签或目标进行下游微调/适配，支持从分子到复杂样品的多种分析任务。

Result: 在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物组分识别与定量、细菌分类、药用植物地理溯源与成分定量、微塑料分类和土壤性质预测等九大类任务中，UltraIR均优于传统机器学习和任务特定的深度学习基线；在标注实验光谱有限的条件下表现强劲，并能在不同FTIR光谱仪和实验室间实现零样本推断。

Conclusion: UltraIR为红外光谱分析提供了一条可适配、数据高效的通用路径，显著改善了复杂真实世界样品中的化学感知能力，展示了基础模型在光谱科学中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Infrared (IR) spectroscopy is widely used for chemical sensing, but extracting reliable chemical information from spectra remains challenging. Conventional interpretation is labor-intensive, relies on prior knowledge and reference spectra, and is difficult to scale, whereas most machine-learning methods are tailored to individual tasks or datasets, require large labeled training sets, and transfer poorly across analytical objectives and experimental datasets. Here we introduce UltraIR, a foundation model for IR spectroscopy with more than 100 million parameters that enables simulation-to-real transfer learning for chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples. UltraIR is pretrained on approximately 60 million simulated IR spectra using spectral reconstruction, molecular fingerprint similarity alignment, and functional-group prediction, then adapted to downstream objectives with task-specific labels or targets. Across functional-group prediction, molecular structure elucidation, physicochemical property prediction, mixture-component identification and quantification, bacterial classification, medicinal-herb geographic origin traceability and constituent quantification, microplastics classification, and soil property prediction, UltraIR outperforms conventional machine-learning and task-specific deep-learning baselines. It performs strongly with limited labeled experimental spectra and in zero-shot inference for the same analytical task across Fourier-transform infrared spectrometers and laboratories, providing a route to adaptable, data-efficient chemical sensing from complex real-world samples.

</details>


### [169] [When Local Variance Optimality Is Not Enough: RoPE-Aligned Q/K Rotations for Dynamic 4-Bit Quantisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13365)
*Shuhan Wang,Yilin Luo,Nan Xu,Chi Wang Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于旋转的后训练量化中，与RoPE兼容的逐对旋转（pairwise rotation）相比全头Hadamard混合的效果。结果表明，在动态W4A4KV4设置下，逐对旋转配置未提升精度，反而在多个检查点上增加困惑度；尽管可以证明其在池化协方差代理目标下达到解析最优，但该代理与量化器的逐token组范围缩放统计不匹配，导致结构化最优并不能降低实际量化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旋转后训练量化方法通常对整个注意力头施加正交变换以减少离群值误差，而RoPE将每个头分割为二维频率对。作者想探究：是否存在一种尊重RoPE这个频率对分解的变换，能够优于全头混合？这需要理论分析和实证验证。

Method: (1) 理论上证明了充分性结论的逆命题：对于不同频率，没有其他单头正交映射能与RoPE交换（commute）；(2) 在头共享参数化下，推导出使较大通道方差最小化的旋转角度（基于池化协方差、位置平均的代理目标），并验证实现达到解析最小值；(3) 在动态W4A4KV4设置下，在四个检查点上对逐对旋转、全头Hadamard及两者组合进行实证对比；(4) 进行从两通道到全头混合的受控插值实验，观察K范围、相对量化误差和困惑度变化。

Result: (1) 逐对旋转配置在所有测试检查点上增加了困惑度，未能优于全头Hadamard；(2) 将与Hadamard组合的逐对旋转可满足±0.05 PPL区间标准；(3) 仅用K估计共享角度在纯逐对配置上能改善每个检查点，但仍与全头混合存在差距；(4) 随着混合支持从两通道扩展到全头，K范围、相对量化误差和困惑度退化均减小；(5) 解析代理目标虽然达到最优，但代理与量化器缩放统计不匹配，解释了理论最优无法转化为实际收益的原因。

Conclusion: 结构化代理目标的解析最优性并不必然转化为量化误差的降低，当代理目标与量化器的尺度设定统计不一致、且变换的混合支持不足时尤其如此。这表明设计RoPE兼容的后训练量化变换时，不能仅依赖与RoPE可交换性这一约束，还需考虑量化器实际的缩放统计和混合支持范围。

Abstract: Rotation-based post-training quantisation commonly applies an orthogonal transform across an entire attention head to reduce outlier-induced error. RoPE instead partitions each head into two-dimensional frequency pairs, raising the question of whether a transform respecting this decomposition can improve on full-head mixing. Prior work has established the per-pair rotations that commute with RoPE. We state the converse result that, for distinct frequencies, no other single-head orthogonal map commutes with RoPE. For the head-shared parameterisation used in our experiments, we then derive the rotation angle that minimises the larger channel variance under a pooled-covariance, position-averaged surrogate and verify that the implementation attains its analytic minimum. The evaluated head-shared pairwise configuration does not improve accuracy in the tested dynamic W4A4KV4 setting. Across four checkpoints, replacing the full-head Hadamard with this configuration increases perplexity at both short and long context lengths. Composing the pairwise rotation with the Hadamard satisfies the selected $\pm0.05$-PPL interval criterion under the default estimator. Estimating the shared angle from K alone improves pairwise-only on every checkpoint but does not close its gap to full-head mixing. The analytic objective controls a position-averaged second moment of a pooled calibration covariance, whereas the dynamic quantiser sets its step from a tokenwise group range. The pairwise transform also has only two-channel mixing support. Along a controlled interpolation from two-channel to full-head mixing, K range, relative quantisation error, and perplexity degradation decrease as support increases. These results show that optimality for a structured surrogate need not reduce quantisation error when the surrogate and mixing support are misaligned with the quantiser's scale-setting statistic.

</details>


### [170] [Reduced Matrix Multiplication: Input-Adaptive Matrix-Product Reduction for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13426)
*Zixuan Lan,Yanhong Li,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RMM（Reduced Matrix Multiplication）的无训练、输入自适应的推理优化方法，通过沿矩阵乘法压缩维度选择信息性切片来降低Transformer推理成本，在不修改模型权重的前提下实现精度与效率的可控权衡，并在1B到70B参数的语言模型及多模态场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型性能优越，但推理时反复进行高维矩阵乘法导致计算成本高昂，现有方法难以在不重新训练或修改权重的条件下实现有效的推理优化。

Method: 提出基于保留率（retention ratio）控制的输入自适应切片选择机制，沿矩阵乘法的压缩维度选取信息量最大的切片，跳过冗余计算，无需训练且不修改模型权重。通过控制保留率实现平滑可预测的精度-效率权衡。

Result: 在1B至70B参数的多种语言模型上，RMM在中等缩减率下保持鲁棒性能；机理消融实验揭示注意力侧计算比MLP组件具有更高的可缩减性；基于自定义核函数的A100实测表明计算节省可转化为实际运行时加速，尤其在长序列场景下显著。

Conclusion: RMM作为一种可扩展的输入自适应推理优化方向，为Transformer推理效率提升提供了有效途径，并验证了其可推广至多模态视觉语言推理场景。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance but incur substantial inference cost due to repeated high-dimensional matrix multiplications. We propose Reduced Matrix Multiplication (RMM), a training-free, input-adaptive inference method that reduces Transformer matrix products by selecting informative slices along their contraction dimensions, without modifying model weights. Under a simple retention-ratio control, RMM provides a smooth and predictable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, we find that reduction tolerance depends on the model family, task, component, and retention ratio, although it often improves with model scale. Under moderate reduction, RMM remains robust across the evaluated discriminative, autoregressive generation, and long-context settings. We further show that the same principle extends to multimodal vision-language inference. Mechanistic ablations reveal a structural asymmetry within Transformers: attention-side computations are substantially more reducible than MLP components. Finally, wall-clock benchmarks with custom kernels on an NVIDIA A100 show that these computational savings can translate into practical runtime gains, especially at longer sequence lengths. Together, these results position RMM as a scalable direction for input-adaptive inference-time optimization.

</details>


### [171] [Symmetry-Breaking De Novo Crystal Generation via Markovian Jump Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13457)
*Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自发对称破缺物理原理的扩散框架SbCD（Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion），通过马尔可夫跳变扩散过程从最低对称性先验出发逆向生成完整晶体结构规格，包括空间群之间的转换，在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上显著优于保留对称性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型只能生成到位点对称性级别，依赖从经验分布中采样空间群，无法捕获全局对称性和结构依赖关系，限制了生成完整晶体学规格的能力。

Method: 受物理中自发对称破缺现象启发，提出基于马尔可夫跳变-扩散过程的扩散框架，从最低对称性先验逆向生成，通过显式建模空间群间转换，实现物理驱动的对称性破缺动力学。

Result: 在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上的从头生成实验中，SbCD显著优于其保留对称性的对照方法，证明显式纳入空间群间转换的有效性。

Conclusion: SbCD为晶体材料的生成式建模提供了有前景的新视角，通过物理启发的对称性破缺机制实现了更完整的晶体结构生成。

Abstract: Generating crystals has recently attracted significant interest due to their broad applications in materials science. However, existing generative models struggle to produce complete crystallographic specifications, limiting their ability to capture global symmetry and structural dependencies. In particular, current state-of-the-art approaches generate crystals only up to site symmetries and rely on sampling space groups from empirical distributions during generation. Inspired by \emph{spontaneous symmetry breaking} in physics, where crystals break symmetries under external conditions, we propose a novel diffusion-based framework that generates full structure specifications by reversing from the lowest-symmetry priors. Our method leverages a Markovian jump-diffusion process to model these symmetry-breaking dynamics, enabling it to traverse different space groups in a physically motivated manner. Our model, dubbed \emph{Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion} (SbCD), introduces a principled approach to explicitly incorporate inter-space-group transitions into the generative process. In de novo generation experiments on MP20 and MPTS-52, SbCD outperforms its symmetry-preserving counterpart by a substantial margin, offering a promising perspective for generative modeling of crystalline materials.

</details>


### [172] [Doubly Robust Estimation of Causal Effect on CVR with Targeted Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13461)
*Jiayi Dan,Bo Li,Lu Deng,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从半参数理论角度重新审视后点击转化率（CVR）的因果效应估计问题，提出了一个新的双重稳健因果效应估计器，专为链式结构结果（如CVR）设计，实现了比冗余参数估计更快的收敛速度，并基于目标正则化构建了实用的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR是电商和广告中的关键指标，但直接在点击样本上应用现有因果推断方法会产生样本选择偏差和方差增大问题。近期研究提出的"ideal loss"方法虽能优化全样本上的无偏损失，但损失的無偏性并不能保证最终估计器的無偏性。因此需要从更根本的理论层面重新设计适用于CVR目标的因果效应估计器。

Method: 从半参数理论出发，针对链式结构结果（如CVR）开发了一个新的双重稳健（doubly robust）因果效应估计器；详细推导了其理论性质，证明其相较于冗余参数估计具有更快的收敛速度，因此在使用灵活的非参数估计器（包括神经网络）时更加鲁棒；在此基础上设计了基于目标正则化（targeted regularization）的框架以提升数值稳定性和实际适用性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的大量实验证明了该方法的有效性和鲁棒性。实验还发现，简单地将损失去偏与标准因果估计器组合的性能不如所提出的方法，这突显了为CVR风格目标开发具有坚实理论保证的新估计器的必要性。

Conclusion: 仅仅关注损失去偏不足以解决CVR因果效应估计问题，需要从半参数理论的层面开发专门针对链式结构结果的因果估计器，以获得更好的理论保证和实际性能。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a key metric in various scenarios including e-commerce and advertising, reflecting the efficiency and user experience in the second stage of the conversion process. Estimating the causal effect on CVR is therefore of great practical importance. However, directly applying existing causal inference methods to clicked samples introduces sample selection bias and increased variance due to the exclusion of non-click data. Recent studies on CVR prediction introduce "ideal loss", which optimizes model parameters using an unbiased estimate of the loss over the full sample. Nevertheless, there is no guarantee that unbiasedness of the loss implies unbiasedness of the final estimator.
  We revisit this challenge from the perspective of semiparametric theory. Specifically, we develop a new doubly robust causal effect estimator for chain-structured outcomes such as CVR, and derive its theoretical properties in detail. It achieves a faster convergence rate compared to nuisance parameters estimation and is therefore more robust when using flexible nonparametric estimators, including neural networks. Based on these theoretical findings, we further design a framework based on targeted regularization to improve numerical stability and practical applicability.
  Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method. In addition, we find that naively combining loss debiasing with standard causal estimators underperforms our method, highlighting the necessity of developing the new estimator tailored to this CVR-style objective with solid theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [173] [Concept Drift Detection and Adaptive Retraining of Malware Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13465)
*Christofer Washington Berruz Chungata,Martin Jurecek,Katerina Potika,William B. Andreopoulos,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究机器学习模型在恶意软件分类中因概念漂移导致的性能退化问题，提出了一种基于One-Class SVM（OCSVM）的自动概念漂移检测方法，并与Minibatch K-Means和MMD方法进行对比。通过四种学习模型和三种重训练场景的大量实验，发现漂移感知的重训练方法在保持与周期性重训练相当的分类精度的同时，大幅提升了训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件检测/分类中的机器学习模型极易因概念漂移（攻击者不断修改恶意软件导致数据分布随时间变化）而性能退化。需要一种自动检测概念漂移的方法，以便在必要时才进行模型重训练，兼顾准确性和效率。

Method: 提出基于OCSVM的漂移检测新方法，与MK-Means和MMD两种基线对比。选用四种学习模型（MLP、随机森林、SVM、XGBoost）和三种场景（静态、周期性重训练、漂移感知重训练）进行实验；并利用Pareto Front分析漂移感知场景下准确率与训练效率之间的权衡。

Result: 三种漂移检测技术在分类准确率上与周期性重训练相当，但在需要重训练的模型数量上显著更高效；其中基于OCSVM的漂移感知重训练方法整体优于MK-Means和MMD。Pareto Front分析结果证实了准确率与效率之间的良好权衡。

Conclusion: 研究提供了有力证据表明可以准确地自动检测恶意软件分类中的概念漂移，且漂移感知的重训练策略能够在保持高分类精度的同时显著降低训练开销，OCSVM方法为最佳选择。

Abstract: Concept drift refers to changes over time in the statistical properties of data, as compared to the data that was used to train a learning model. Machine learning models for malware detection or classification are particularly susceptible to performance degradation caused by concept drift, as attackers constantly modify existing malware. In this chapter, we analyze two machine learning-based approaches to automated concept drift detection-a novel approach based on One-Class Support Vector Machines (OCSVM) and a previously-studied technique based on Minibatch K-Means (MK-Means). For comparison we also consider Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), a statistical technique for detecting changes in multidimensional data. We conduct an extensive series of experiments comparing the effectiveness of four learning models, namely, Multilayer Perceptron, Random Forest, Support Vector Machines, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting. For each of these models, we consider three distinct scenarios: A static scenario where no model retraining occurs, a periodic scenario where models are constantly retrained irrespective of concept drift, and a drift-aware scenario where models are only retrained when concept drift is detected. Under the drift-aware scenario, we analyze the tradeoff between accuracy and training efficiency using Pareto Front analysis. We find that all three concept drift detection techniques achieve classification accuracy comparable to periodic retraining, while offering substantially greater efficiency in terms of the number of models that must be retrained. In addition, drift-aware retraining based on our OCSVM technique generally outperforms the MK-Means and MMD approaches. Overall, these results provide strong evidence that we can accurately detect concept drift in malware classification models.

</details>


### [174] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非光滑复合目标分布的Moreau-Yosida未调整Langevin算法（MYULA），提出用参考活跃迹（reference active trace）替代全局曲率界来控制MYULA的离散化误差，从而获得更精细的迭代复杂度估计，并给出端到端的收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MYULA收敛分析依赖于全局曲率界d/λ，这在高维或小平滑参数λ时过于保守，导致迭代复杂度估计过于悲观。本文旨在通过更精细的量（参考活跃迹）刻画MYULA的离散化误差，以获得更紧的收敛性分析。

Method: 定义g_λ为g的Moreau包络，π_λ为平滑化目标分布，a_λ为g_λ的Hessian迹。证明MYULA主导离散化误差由参考活跃迹B_ref控制（即从π_λ出发进行一次MYULA热子步的a_λ平均值），而非全局曲率界d/λ。进一步给出Moreau偏差界，通过对特殊结构惩罚（分段线性、Lasso、组、全变差）进行曲率-管道估计，使得B_ref与λ无关。

Result: 得到两个核心结果：(1) MYULA在W_2度量下的迭代复杂度，由B_ref和M_λ（a_λ的a.e.上界）刻画，通用界B_ref≤d/λ给出Õ(ε^{-3})精度依赖；(2) Moreau偏差界√m W_2(π_λ,π)≤G²λ/4，选择λ≍ε/G²可得到端到端保证。对结构化的分段线性等惩罚，曲率-管道估计使B_ref与λ无关，从而对同一经典MYULA核得到Õ(ε^{-2})精度依赖。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入参考活跃迹这一更精细的量，显著改进了非光滑复合目标下MYULA的收敛性分析，将通用界的ε^{-3}复杂度改进为结构化问题的ε^{-2}，且无需修改算法本身。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [175] [Synthetic Persona Pretraining: Alignment from Token Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13482)
*Julian Minder,Viktor Moskvoretskii,Raghav Singhal,Difan Jiao,Andy Arditi,Shaobo Cui,Yiderigun Borjigin,Kartik Bali,Stefan Krsteski,Harsh Raj,Huu Nguyen,Jannik Brinkmann,Ashton Anderson,Roland Aydin,Robert West*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出合成人格预训练（SPP），在预训练第一步就将期望的助手人格从token零开始植入模型，通过注释价值一致的反思文本、标准交叉熵预训练、以及后期对话数据绑定人格三步流程，在高达3B参数、500B token的模型上验证了该方法能提升宪法遵循、增强越狱鲁棒性、降低分布外道德困境中的错位率，同时保持模型能力不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的对齐通常在预训练之后才引入，这导致对齐价值观只是浅层覆盖而非深层植入，容易引发后续的错位（misalignment）问题。作者旨在探索一种全新范式，在预训练阶段就从根本上植入期望的助手人格，使价值观成为模型的固有根基而非事后叠加。

Method: SPP方法包括三步：1）基于规范性价值宪法，为预训练文档标注带有价值对齐的第一人称反思文本；2）使用标准交叉熵损失同时在标准预训练文档及其反思文本上进行预训练，在众多人格中植入期望人格；3）在用户-助手对话数据上进行后训练，将期望人格绑定到助手身份上，即"人格绑定"（persona binding）过程。

Result: 在高达3B参数、500B token的预训练规模下，SPP提升了宪法遵循能力和越狱鲁棒性，降低了分布外道德困境中的错位率，同时保持模型能力不受损。消融实验表明，延迟引入SPP（仅在预训练末尾加入）效果显著减弱：宪法遵循更弱、价值优先级未发生偏移、道德困境中选择更不对齐。且SPP的优势依赖于人格绑定，并随预训练预算增加而增强。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，早期塑造价值观对对齐至关重要，预训练阶段的人格干预是有效实现深层价值观对齐的可行途径。

Abstract: As language-model-based AI is increasingly deployed in autonomous settings, aligning its goals and values with those of humans becomes critical. Today, alignment, and the assistant identity itself, are typically introduced only after pretraining, once behavioral priors are already established. This can make values a thin overlay, rather than deeply rooted, and facilitate subsequent misalignment. Pursuing a different paradigm, we introduce Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP), which installs the desired assistant persona from token zero in pretraining. First, we annotate pretraining documents with value-aligned first-person reflections derived from a normative value constitution. Second, we pretrain via the standard cross-entropy loss on standard pretraining documents as well as their reflections, which installs the desired persona among a multitude of other personas. Finally, we post-train on user-assistant dialogue data, which binds this desired persona to the assistant identity, a process we call persona binding. By pretraining models up to 3B parameters on 500B tokens, we show that SPP improves constitution following and jailbreak robustness, and reduces the misalignment rate in out-of-distribution moral dilemmas, while preserving capabilities. Early intervention matters: compared with alignment from token zero, introducing SPP only at the end of pretraining yields weaker constitution adherence, does not shift value priorities, and leads to less aligned choices in dilemmas. This advantage depends on persona binding and, importantly, increases with pretraining budget. Overall, our results show that shaping values early is critical for alignment and establish pretraining-time persona interventions as an effective approach to do so.

</details>


### [176] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏正交回归技术（SORT），一种基于L1正则化回归的稀疏谱框架，用于从噪声和不规则采样的数据中学习正交基展开系数，主要应用于数据驱动的微分方程发现，并通过系数读出支持非线性逼近和高维积分估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归、文法发现和SINDy类方法依赖有限函数库进行稀疏识别，当库中缺少问题特定的非线性项时模型容易失败。作者希望将问题从对通用项的脆弱选择转变为适应问题域的基设计，从而提供更稳健的可复用中间表示。

Method: SORT直接从观测数据中通过L1正则化回归估计展开系数，避免显式数值积分或解析内积计算。将向量场表示为所选正交基上的稀疏系数展开，通过稀疏回归实现基系数恢复。

Result: 在动力系统实验中，当基适应问题时SORT匹配或优于基于库的稀疏回归基线；在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示不匹配情况下表现出更稳定的退化。主导低阶系数随模型阶数增加而持续存在，支持阶一致模型增长。

Conclusion: SORT提供了一个可复用的中间表示，用于系统辨识、逼近和积分估计，将基设计显式纳入科学建模问题，弥补了基于通用函数库方法的脆弱性。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [177] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍Intern-S2-Preview系列科学智能体基础模型，通过科学多模态预训练和统一的后训练流程（SFT、多任务RL、代理RL、蒸馏），构建397B参数模型，在科学、多模态、智能体和通用基准上取得竞争性或领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现需要AI系统能够跨异质模态推理科学证据、与科学工具和环境交互，并在长任务时间跨度上持续进步。现有模型缺乏针对科学场景的统一训练范式。

Method: 训练流程包括：(1) 科学多模态预训练（渲染的科技文档、交错图文数据、多样科学语料）；(2) 统一后训练流程（SFT、可扩展多任务RL、黑盒/白盒代理RL、在线策略蒸馏）；(3) 提升稳定性和效率的实用技术（部分rollout加off-policy校正、自适应长度正则化、在线投机解码、稳健多任务优化、trace感知经验组装）；(4) 架构上增加了时间序列建模模块和独立的Memory Decoder记忆增强路径。

Result: Intern-S2-Preview-397B在科学、多模态、智能体和通用基准上取得竞争性/领先结果。时间序列模块提升SciTS上的科学信号理解和预测能力；独立的Intern-MemDec-4B扩展将Biology-Instructions平均分从56.92提升至60.32，且无需修改冻结的397B骨干网络。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了科学智能体基础模型的有效训练范式，并通过时间序列建模和记忆增强模块分别提升了科学信号处理能力和快速科学领域专业化能力。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [178] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种干预感知的临床世界模型（intervention-aware clinical world model），将患者表示为结构化潜在状态，并通过时间排序的术后干预事件来演化该状态。模型在DECAAF-II心房颤动消融数据集上验证，90天恢复窗口内的不规则术后记录为长期复发风险提供临床证据，AUROC达0.756，AUPRC达0.777，疤痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测模型将术后结局视为从基线测量到未来终点的单步映射，忽略了恢复过程中异步记录的多类信息（临床观察、用药变化、重复干预、生理测量）对风险随时间的影响。

Method: 首先将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态；随后利用手术上下文、静态协变量、经过时间和围事件生理嵌入来更新该状态；随访影像仅作为训练阶段的潜在预测监督信号。

Result: 在DECAAF-II反复内部交叉验证中，复发预测AUROC为0.756、AUPRC为0.777；疤痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点，且在推理时无需随访MRI数据。学习到的状态支持不同预测时域下的复发风险查询及空白期记录的回顾性输入编辑。

Conclusion: 该模型验证了时序化手术期事件对长期结局预测的价值，且具备灵活的推理能力，适用于不规律随访记录的真实临床场景。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [179] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散采样的掩码扩散（masking diffusion），提出了一种名为"解掩码生长复杂度"（UGC）的路径解析数据几何度量。UGC的局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，统一了Bernoulli子集和固定基数解掩码方案的分析。基于此提出了优化调度、可从样本估计UGC增量，并实现了具有保证精度和接近最优迭代复杂度的"认证最优"采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型的采样质量取决于离散化调度方案（schedule），但现有分析缺乏一个统一的几何框架来刻画数据结构如何影响KL离散化误差，难以为不同掩码方案设计最优调度并量化自适应计算带来的增益。

Method: 1) 定义UGC（解掩码生长复杂度）作为路径解析的数据几何度量；2) 在log-reveal-odds坐标下推导单块与多块的最优调度；3) 利用KL增量沿耦合揭示轨迹估计UGC增量，构建certified-optimal采样器；4) 通过聚合UGC路径建立与经典度量依赖及先验复杂度测量的联系；5) 分析细划分极限下的最优Euler离散化误差。

Result: 提出了统一的离散化误差分析框架；实现了高概率保证指定KL误差且迭代复杂度在常数因子内接近oracle过程的采样器；在细划分极限下得到平方根UGC密度的平方积分决定最优Euler误差；通过常数个自适应放置的块可在某些例子中实现维度相关的显著改进，包括Ω̃(√d)的提升。

Conclusion: UGC为离散采样中的掩码扩散提供了一种统一且可估计的几何度量，能够指导调度优化并实现认证最优的采样算法，揭示了将计算资源配置到数据几何结构上所带来的显著维度增益。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [180] [Vero: Can AI Agents Build Formally Verified Software Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13522)
*Zhe Ye,Hantao Lou,Yuechun Sun,Peiyang Song,Zhengxu Yan,Timothe Kasriel,Qingyang Zhang,Kaiyu Yang,Soonho Kong,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Vero，首个在仓库级别评估AI智能体联合完成代码实现与形式化证明合成的基准。包含43个来自真实世界的多模块Lean 4实例，覆盖Python、Dafny、Verus和Coq等语言。当前最强智能体仅完全解决27/43实例，在最难仓库中未能关闭任何规范，表明现有能力仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么只关注单个函数，要么仅评估给定实现下的证明生成，无法衡量智能体在真实多模块代码库中协调实现与证明设计决策的能力。这一缺口阻碍了可信AI生成软件的发展。

Method: 构建Vero基准：收集43个多模块实例（源自真实仓库，涵盖Python、Dafny、Verus、Coq，涉及密码协议到分布式系统等域），每个实例包含Lean 4仓库、预定API接口、人工整理的形式化规范及参考实现；支持纯证明与代码+证明两种评估模式；引入审计机制允许智能体正式证明规范不可满足性或参考代码不正确，用于暴露和修正策展过程中的潜在错误。

Result: 评估前沿编码智能体配置（具备Lean工具链访问权限）。最强智能体完全解决43个实例中的27个，在难度最高的仓库上未关闭任何规范，表明即便是最先进智能体在仓库级验证代码合成上仍能力不足。

Conclusion: Vero为衡量仓库级验证软件合成的进展提供了具体测试平台，揭示了当前智能体在该任务上仍明显不足，并公开了基准、策展流程和评估工具以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for programming, but do not provide any guarantee on the correctness of generated code. Verified code generation, in which an agent produces both an implementation and a machine-checked proof of its specification, offers a stronger path toward trustworthy AI-generated software. Existing benchmarks in this direction either focus on individual functions or only evaluate proof generation with provided implementations. It is still an open question whether agents can make coherent implementation and proof choices across real multi-module codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce Vero, the first benchmark to evaluate joint implementation and proof synthesis at the repository level. Vero contains 43 multi-module instances sourced from real-world repositories spanning Python, Dafny, Verus, and Coq, and covering diverse domains from cryptographic protocols to distributed systems. Each instance consists of a multi-module Lean 4 repository with predetermined API interfaces, manually curated formal specifications, and reference implementations, supporting both proof-only and code-and-proof evaluation modes. To improve benchmark reliability, Vero also includes an audit mechanism where agents are allowed to formally prove unsatisfiability of provided specification or incorrectness of reference code, which surfaces and corrects latent code and specification errors during curation. We evaluate frontier coding-agent configurations with Lean toolchain access. The strongest agent fully solves only 27 of 43 instances and closes no specifications on the hardest repositories. Vero provides a concrete testbed for measuring progress toward repository-scale verified software synthesis, where current agents still fall short. We release the benchmark, curation pipeline, and evaluation harness at https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/vero.

</details>


### [181] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DARTree，一种无需训练的投机解码方法，将预训练的自回归（AR）校正头从链式扩展为树结构，通过单批次扩充分数宽度候选树并仅使用最佳优先剪枝选择验证树，解耦AR头推理与顺序堆操作。在七个基准、四种模型-温度配置下，平均接受长度和加速比均达最优，单轮最多接受12.97个token，比DFlash高98.6%，比Domino高27.9%，实现最高9.73倍无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码通过并行验证多个草稿token来无损加速自回归语言模型。扩散式草稿器可并行预测整个token块，但其位置分布是边缘分布而非沿草稿路径的条件分布。现有递归校正仅在单一草稿链上引入因果信息，而基于扩散的树构建虽拓宽了候选覆盖，却未沿各分支进行校正。为同时获得树覆盖广度和逐分支因果校正，需要将AR校正头从链扩展至树。

Method: DARTree首先构建固定宽度的候选树，通过单批次展开并评分每一深度的所有节点；随后仅应用最佳优先剪枝来选择验证树，从而将AR头推理与顺序堆操作解耦。该方法无需训练，直接扩展预训练AR校正头的适用范围。

Result: 在七个数学、代码和聊天基准测试中，DARTree在全部四种模型-温度配置下均获得最高的平均接受长度和加速比。单轮验证最多接受12.97个token，较DFlash提升98.6%，较Domino提升27.9%，最高达到9.73倍的无损加速（相对本地测量的自回归解码）。

Conclusion: DARTree有效结合了树结构候选覆盖的广度与AR校正头的逐分支因果信息，在不增加训练成本的前提下显著提升了投机解码的接受长度与加速性能，验证了将AR校正头从链式推广到树式的有效性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [182] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多标签分类与二值分割中Jaccard分数（IoU）的校准与代理损失问题。证明Jaccard损失矩阵非奇异，其列仿射维度为2^s-1；精确校准需要2^{s-1}到2^s-1的校准维度，即任何精确校准的凸代理需要指数级预测坐标。然而，对于任意固定加性遗憾容差，通过MinHash平方损失代理（带符号变体）可实现多项式维度的近似校准保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard分数/IoU是多标签分类和二值分割中的标准评估指标，但其在损失校准层面的理论性质（如损失矩阵奇异性、校准维度、代理损失的维度需求）尚未被系统研究。理解精确校准与近似校准对预测坐标维度的要求，对于设计高效可靠的代理损失算法具有重要的理论与实践意义。

Method: 1) 使用有限MinHash Gram表示结合布尔Möbius反演，证明Jaccard、平移损失及普通损失矩阵的非奇异性和列仿射维度。2) 通过因子加权分布与Bayes最优报告构造下界，证明CCdim(L^Jac)的下界为2^{s-1}。3) 提出F1-to-Jaccard回归转移，利用现有(s^2+1)维F1代理获得多项式时间的渐近Jaccard遗憾上界。4) 设计MinHash平方损失代理（直接构造与带符号变体），给出概率至少1-ρ下遗憾不超过α的维度上界。

Result: 证明了Jaccard损失矩阵非奇异且列仿射维度为2^s-1；精确校准的CCdim在[2^{s-1}, 2^s-1]范围内。F1-to-Jaccard转移给出渐近Jaccard遗憾≤3-2√2≈0.1716的多项式时间规则。MinHash平方损失代理的维度上界为O((s^2+s log(1/ρ))/α^2)（直接构造）和O((s+log(1/ρ))/α^2)（带符号变体），在任意条件标签分布下均匀成立。结论：零遗憾校准需指数维度，而任意固定加性遗憾容差均只需多项式预测维度。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了Jaccard分数在损失校准中的维度复杂度：精确校准不可避免需要指数级预测坐标，而近似校准（固定遗憾容差）可通过MinHash平方损失实现多项式维度。这揭示了零遗憾精确代理与固定容差异度近似之间的指数-多项式维度鸿沟，为在实际应用中权衡预测维度与遗憾性能提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [183] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Defensive Booster的防御性预测算法，同时获得在线梯度提升（在Brier分数上竞争保证）和在线弱到强分类提升（在弱学习条件下误差归零）两种保障，并通过"弱学习条件的ex-post硬核证明"来操作化boosting的双重视角。该算法只需访问一个弱类学习器，效率极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升技术只能分别提供两种不可比较的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上与H的跨度竞争，但跨度中不包含准确预测器时无任何保证；在线弱到强分类提升在弱学习条件下将误差驱动到零，但条件不满足时保证很弱。缺少一个同时获得这两种保证的算法。

Method: 提出Defensive Booster算法，其核心思想是操作化提升的"双重视角"：当算法的随机化分类误差持续较高时，其错误权重形成一种平滑重加权，在该重加权下所有弱假设的低边缘（low edge）构成弱学习条件失败的ex-post硬核证明（hard-core certificate）。算法只需调用一个弱类学习器，并包含一个强自适应变体以在任意时间区间上同时满足两种保证。

Result: 在任意自适应序列上，Brier分数与在线梯度提升以相同速率竞争。同时，当实际转录满足平滑弱学习条件时，其Brier分数和随机化分类误差与在线分类提升达到相同速率保证。实验表明在合成和真实数据流上预测性能强（有时显著优于所有先前基线），且运行速度快数个数量级。

Conclusion: Defensive Booster是首个同时获得在线梯度提升和在线分类提升两种不可比较保证的算法，且比现有在线提升方法高效得多（仅需一个弱类学习器而非大集成）。这为在线概率预测提供了一种统一的防御性预测方案。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [SCALE-Sim EVA: Design Principles for an Extensible, Visualizable, and Adaptable Accelerator Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12354)
*Jingtian Dang,Ritik Raj,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了SCALE-Sim EVA，一个可扩展、可可视化、可适配的IR感知加速器仿真框架，基于张量就绪度和硬件单元可用性计算周期时序，通过命令分解桥接编译器级IR粒度与硬件级执行粒度，并支持开放trace的可视化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI加速器日益异构化，包含混合计算单元、层级化存储、局部缓冲和专用数据移动路径，导致固定仿真器难以扩展以适配超出其原始执行模型的场景。

Method: EVA将工作负载表示为基于张量的命令，跟踪运行时张量放置与就绪状态，在可组合的硬件组件（用户自定义功能单元和存储行为）上执行命令。它不重放地址级周期trace，而是根据张量就绪度、硬件单元可用性和建模的操作/传输延迟来计算周期时序。命令分解机制将编译器级IR粒度的全局张量操作降级并调度到硬件层级结构上，同时输出开放的命令和存储trace用于时间线、存储占用和资源竞争的可视化分析。

Result: EVA实现了可扩展的异质硬件建模能力，通过张量就绪驱动的时序计算替代地址级trace重放，并提供了IR到硬件层级的命令分解调度机制以及开放trace的可视化分析能力。

Conclusion: EVA为AI加速器仿真提供了一个可扩展、可可视化且可适配的框架，能有效应对异构硬件多样性带来的仿真拓展挑战。

Abstract: Modern AI accelerators increasingly combine heterogeneous compute units, hierarchical memories, local buffers, and specialized data movement paths. This diversity makes fixed accelerator simulators difficult to extend beyond their original execution model. We present SCALE-Sim EVA, an extensible, visualizable, and adaptable simulation framework for IR-aware accelerator modeling. EVA represents workloads as tensor-based commands, tracks runtime tensor placement and readiness, and executes commands on composable hardware components with user-defined functional units and memory behavior. Instead of replaying address-level cycle traces, EVA computes cycle timing from tensor readiness, hardware-unit availability, and modeled operation or transfer latency. Its command decomposition mechanism bridges compiler-level IR granularity and hardware-level execution granularity, allowing global tensor operations to be lowered and scheduled locally across a hardware hierarchy. EVA also emits open command and storage traces for visual analysis of execution timelines, memory occupancy, and resource contention.

</details>


### [185] [Lonic: Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design for Energy-Efficient Fully Local Online SNN Training with INT4 Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12500)
*Peilin Chen,Xiaoxuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Lonic是一个用于全局部在线监督脉冲神经网络（SNN）学习的算法-硬件协同设计方案，通过INT4低精度训练算法与重构的乘法器-free整数PE阵列等硬件优化，在保持精度的同时显著提升训练能效和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN局部在线训练算法虽然解决了内存和计算开销问题，但未考虑算法优势能否有效转化为真实设备效率。缺乏从算法-硬件协同角度来验证和实现这些算法在实际硬件上的能效提升。

Method: 算法侧采用INT4低精度训练算法实现全局部在线SNN学习；硬件侧设计了可重构的乘法器-free整数PE阵列、双优化零门控策略、时间前缀加速的局部学习数据流以及低精度权重移动方案，通过算法与硬件的协同设计来提升训练效率。

Result: 相比Apple M4和Nvidia V100 GPU，Lonic分别实现平均17.44x和66.28x的能效提升，以及3.25x和1.02x的加速。相比ASIC TPU-like和H2Learn加速器，分别实现15.95x（14.64x）和1.52x（7.28x）的能效（面积效率）提升。

Conclusion: Lonic通过算法-硬件协同设计，成功将SNN全局部在线训练算法的理论优势转化为实际硬件效率，实现了显著的能效和速度提升，为SNN在实际设备上的节能训练提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have recently attracted increasing attention as an energy-efficient learning paradigm. Existing works also propose temporally and fully local online SNN training algorithms to address memory and computation overhead. However, they do not consider whether the algorithmic advantages can be effectively translated into real-device efficiency. To address this challenge, we present Lonic, an algorithm-hardware co-design for energy-efficient and scalable fully local online supervised SNN learning. On the algorithm side, we implement an INT4 low-precision training algorithm for fully local online SNN learning while maintaining accuracy. On the hardware side, to leverage the benefits of the proposed algorithm, we introduce reconfigurable multiplier-free integer PE arrays, dual-optimization zero-gating strategy, temporal prefix-accelerated local learning dataflow, and low-precision weight movement to significantly improve training efficiency. Compared to Apple M4 and Nvidia V100 GPUs, Lonic achieves average energy efficiency improvements of 17.44x and 66.28x, respectively, along with speedups of 3.25x and 1.02x, respectively. Moreover, Lonic achieves 15.95x (14.64x) and 1.52x (7.28x) energy efficiency (area efficiency) over ASIC TPU-like and H2Learn accelerators, respectively. The code for Lonic is available at https://github.com/peilin-chen/Lonic.

</details>


### [186] [GateTruth: Auditing the Rigor of RTL Design Benchmarks via Mutation Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12635)
*Meet Bhadra*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出GateTruth，一个基于变异测试（mutation testing）的引擎和方法论，用于审计RTL设计基准测试中testbench的严谨性。作者对自家68任务基准套件进行验证，46/60的Track A testbench能杀死至少95%的注入突变体；同时将引擎应用于外部基准RTLLM v2.0，发现72%的可审计设计低于95%阈值，3个设计得分为0%。作者还发现输出token上限会暗中截断模型输出并影响排名，主张变异杀死认证应成为RTL生成基准的标准报告要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RTL硬件设计大模型基准测试快速涌现，但没有任何基准报告应用变异测试来量化自身testbench的质量。一个从不失败的testbench并不能证明设计正确——它可能根本没有刺激到真正有缺陷的逻辑。因此需要一个系统性的方法来审计基准测试中testbench的严谨性和可信度。

Method: 作者提出GateTruth引擎：向参考设计中注入确定性的、带种子的语义变异体（mutants），测量testbench能捕获其中多大比例。作者构建了包含60个spec-to-RTL生成任务和8个agentic修复任务的双轨参考套件，使用固定的确定性综合到时序流程进行评分，以正确性作为严格门控。随后将同一引擎不加修改地应用于外部基准RTLLM v2.0进行横向审计。

Result: 在自家套件中，46/60的Track A testbench能杀死至少95%的注入突变体（顺序、可重现执行条件下），其余14个testbench的失败原因被披露（包括Goodhart效应，即testbench为通过门控而被修订）；对RTLLM v2.0的审计中，46个可审计设计中有72%低于95%阈值，3个得分为0%；对NVIDIA CVDP基准的结构性审计不可行（未公开参考解）；还发现4096-token输出上限暗中截断3/7个模型，提升到16384 tokens后一个模型从第5名升至第1名。

Conclusion: 变异杀死认证（mutation-kill certification）应成为RTL生成基准的标准化报告要求。基准测试的testbench质量必须被量化审计，否则基准分数本身可能反映的是testbench的盲区而非模型的真实能力。

Abstract: Benchmarks for evaluating large language models on register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware design have proliferated rapidly, yet none reports having applied mutation testing, an established hardware-verification technique for quantifying testbench quality, to ask whether its own testbenches are trustworthy. A testbench that never fails is not evidence of a correct design; it may simply never stimulate the logic that is actually broken. We introduce GateTruth, a mutation-testing engine and methodology for auditing RTL benchmark testbench rigor: inject a deterministic, seeded set of semantic mutants into a reference design and measure what fraction the testbench catches.
  We validate the methodology against our own 68-task, dual-track reference suite -- 60 specification-to-RTL generation tasks and 8 agentic-repair tasks, scored through a pinned, deterministic synthesis-to-timing flow with correctness enforced as a strict gate -- certifying that 46 of 60 Track A testbenches kill at least 95% of injected mutants under sequential, reproducible execution; we disclose why the other 14 do not, including a Goodhart effect on testbenches revised to pass this gate. We then point the same engine, unmodified, at RTLLM v2.0, a widely adopted external benchmark: of 46 auditable designs, 72% fall below the 95% floor our own suite is held to, and three score 0% outright.
  A comparable audit of NVIDIA's CVDP benchmark is structurally impossible: its public release withholds reference solutions, removing the golden RTL mutation testing requires. Auditing our own instrument also surfaced a second finding: an initially uniform 4096-token output cap silently truncated three of seven evaluated models, and re-running at 16,384 tokens moved one model from fifth place to first. We argue mutation-kill certification should become a standard reporting requirement for RTL-generation benchmarks generally.

</details>


### [187] [Spec-Driven Hardware Evolution via Executable Contract Refinement and Proof-Guided RTL Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12684)
*Shibo Zhao,Yang Zhang,Mengxia Tao,Baoqi Zhang,Kezhi Li,Qiang Xu,Binwu Zhu,Hao Yan,Min Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"基于规范的硬件演化"（spec-driven hardware evolution）框架，将RTL版本迭代从直接的prompt-to-RTL生成转变为契约驱动的四阶段流程（Specify、Plan、Implement、Validate），通过可执行契约、跨版本语义差异分析和证明引导检查实现受信任遗留设计的版本演化，并在TPU数据路径数据格式变更案例中验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件开发本质上是演化式的——重大版本修订通常始于改变预期行为，然后在已验证实现的基础上进行更新，而非从头重写RTL。然而现有基于LLM的硬件研究大多将任务框定为prompt-to-RTL生成，对受信任遗留设计的语义版本演化支持有限。

Method: 提出契约中心的RTL版本迭代框架：将新功能请求精炼为可审查的可执行契约（包含行为级参考模型及显式观察与检查语义），而非直接作为RTL生成的提示词。整个硬件演化分为四阶段：Specify（契约批准）、Plan（基于变异语义探测推导跨版本语义差异并定位受影响RTL区域）、Implement与Validate（在证明引导检查和迭代修复下进行遗留感知的RTL更新）。

Result: 在代表性的TPU数据路径模块的数据格式变更控制版本演化案例研究中验证了框架，结果支持契约驱动硬件演化的可行性，证明后端工作流能在可审查可执行契约下有效驱动已验证遗留RTL实现向下一版本功能收敛。

Conclusion: 契约驱动的硬件演化是可行的，四阶段工作流能够将LLM从原始RTL生成转向语义驱动的遗留代码演化，为硬件版本迭代提供了更符合工程实践的范式；匿名可复现制品已公开。

Abstract: Hardware development is inherently evolutionary: major revisions typically begin by changing intended behavior and then updating a previously validated implementation, rather than regenerating RTL from scratch. Yet most recent LLM-based hardware research still frames the task primarily as prompt-to-RTL generation, offering limited support for semantic version evolution of trusted legacy designs. We present spec-driven hardware evolution, a contract-centered formulation for RTL version iteration. Instead of treating a new feature request as a direct prompt for RTL generation, we refine it into a reviewed executable contract for the next version. This contract specifies what must hold at the externally visible transactional level through a behavior-level reference together with explicit observation and checking semantics, while leaving how the change is realized in RTL to the evolution process. Based on this formulation, we organize hardware evolution into four stages: Specify, Plan, Implement, and Validate. After contract approval, the remaining stages proceed automatically: Plan derives cross-version semantic deltas and localizes affected RTL regions, aided by mutation-based semantic probing; Implement and Validate then perform legacy-aware RTL update under proof-guided checking and iterative repair. We evaluate the framework on a controlled version-evolution case study of a representative TPU datapath block under data-format changes. The results support the feasibility of contract-driven hardware evolution and demonstrate that the proposed backend workflow can effectively drive validated legacy RTL toward next-version functional convergence under a reviewed executable contract. An anonymous artifact for reproducibility is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SDHE-3A6C.

</details>


### [188] [SynAct: A Reasoning-Acting Large Language Model Agent for Adaptive Synthesis Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12751)
*Fangzhou Liu,Peiyi Han,Jiawei Liu,Yuan Pu,Zhuolun He,Rongliang Fu,Tsung-Yi Ho,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SynAct是一个基于LLM推理-行动机制的闭环自适应综合优化智能体，通过迭代诊断实时综合报告、推理当前电路状态、检索工具知识及历史优化经验来发布针对性命令，在14个设计上将平均WNS降至自举综合的27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑综合的PPA结果对优化命令选择高度敏感，综合调优维度高且代价昂贵。现有方法存在两类局限：自动化方法在固定动作空间内进行黑盒搜索，缺乏决策级可解释性；基于LLM的方法通常一次性生成静态脚本，无法适应不断演化的电路状态。

Method: SynAct采用自适应闭环的LLM推理-行动（reasoning-acting）智能体架构，迭代执行以下流程：诊断实时综合报告，结合当前电路状态、检索到的EDA工具知识以及历史优化经验进行多源推理，然后发出针对性的综合优化命令。优化目标聚焦于改善时序（尤其WNS），同时维持面积和功耗的平衡。

Result: 在商业综合工具上对14个设计进行实验，SynAct将平均最差负裕量（WNS）降低至bootstrap综合结果的27%。

Conclusion: SynAct通过闭环自适应推理-行动机制有效解决了传统方法无法根据电路实时状态动态调整优化策略的缺陷，显著改善了综合结果中的时序表现，验证了LLM驱动的自适应综合优化的可行性。

Abstract: Logic synthesis transforms RTL designs into gate-level netlists, where PPA results are highly sensitive to the choice of optimization commands, making synthesis tuning both high-dimensional and expensive. Previous approaches fall into two categories: automated methods, which perform black-box search over fixed action spaces with limited decision-level interpretability, and LLM-based methods, which typically generate static scripts upfront and cannot adapt to evolving circuit states. We present SynAct, an adaptive closed-loop LLM reasoning--acting agent that iteratively diagnoses live synthesis reports and reasons over the current circuit state, retrieved tool knowledge, and historical optimization experience to issue targeted commands. SynAct focuses on improving timing, particularly worst negative slack (WNS), while maintaining balanced area and power trade-offs. Experiments on a commercial synthesis tool across 14 designs show that SynAct reduces average WNS to 27% of that from bootstrap synthesis.

</details>


### [189] [Dryas: A Reprogrammable Engine for High-Speed Interconnect Tracing and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12934)
*Manuel Bröchin,Tom Kuchler,Michael Giardino,David Cock,Timothy Roscoe*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Dryas，一个开源的可重构追踪过滤工具，用于分析和调试现代高性能互连（如30 GiB/s、200ns延迟的CPU-FPGA互连）。该工具基于非确定性有限自动机（NFA）和状态转移元素（STE），以极小的硬件资源开销实现缓存行粒度的全速事件检测，并支持亚秒级动态重配置过滤器而无需重新编程FPGA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算系统中的异构组件带来了高带宽低延迟互连，但这些接口和协议极其复杂，FPGA实现的开发、调试和分析需要大量工程工作。一旦功能实现完成，优化控制器和相关软件需要处理数百GB的追踪数据，缺乏高效的分析工具。

Method: 基于运行时可重构的覆盖引擎（overlay engine）构建，核心采用NFA和STE实现高效的状态转移过滤。该引擎以缓存行粒度追踪事件，支持在不重新编程FPGA或不干扰运行中应用的前提下，在不到一秒内更换过滤器。作者首先研究了NFA的数学基础，然后在真实的相干CPU-FPGA研究平台上实现并评估其可扩展性。

Result: 在真实相干CPU-FPGA平台上实现了Dryas，验证了其在不同规模NFA下的可扩展性，并通过两个用例（调试FPGA互连实现和分析缓存行为）展示了其在实际场景中的有效性。该工具使用极少的硬件资源即可在满速运行下发现罕见、复杂或短暂的事件。

Conclusion: Dryas提供了一种高效的可重构互连追踪分析方案，能够以极小的资源开销实现在线、全速的复杂事件检测，显著简化了高性能互连的调试与应用程序行为分析流程。

Abstract: The proliferation of heterogeneous components in modern computing systems has been accompanied by new higher bandwidth and lower latency interconnects. These interfaces and protocols are enormously complex and the process of developing, debugging, and analyzing FPGA-based implementations requires significant engineering work. Moreover, once a functional implementation is completed, optimization of the controller and associated software requires processing potentially hundreds of gigabytes of trace data.
  In this paper, we present Dryas, an open source tool for analyzing such an interconnect. We developed our tool, using minimal hardware resources, alongside an FPGA implementation of a very high speed, low latency (30~GiB/s, 200~ns) interconnect. With our run-time reprogrammable overlay engine we can inspect this interconnect to find rare, complex, or transient events even at full operation. This filtering engine is based on non-deterministic finite automata (NFAs), efficiently implemented using state transition elements (STEs), allowing us to trace events at a cache-line granularity. Moreover we can change the filters in less than a second, without reprogramming the FPGA or interfering with the running application. This data enables not only debugging the implementation of the interconnect itself, but analyzing the behavior of accelerated applications.
  We examine the mathematical basis for using NFAs and describe their implementation on a real coherent CPU-FPGA research platform. We then evaluate the scalability of Dryas for various size NFAs, followed by two different use cases: debugging FPGA implementation of the interconnect and analyzing cache behavior.

</details>


### [190] [Why Do Prefetchers Fail? Let Agents Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13027)
*Xiangfeng Sun,Ceyu Xu,Ningzhi Ai,Zeyu Zhu,Yiyang Yuan,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种性能异常驱动的自动研究流程，通过迭代诊断硬件预取器（prefetcher）的失效原因，自动合成了"预取器混合体"（MoP）。该系统利用LLM代理分析程序计数器定位高影响未命中、验证诊断并生成专用子预取器，在SPEC CPU2006/2017上取得61.1%的IPC加速比，超越了多个人工设计的SOTA预取器，并完成了6nm工艺下的RTL综合验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件预取器的设计高度依赖人工专家经验，需要人工检查数十亿指令的trace、识别访问模式并将其转化为硬件启发式算法，过程劳动密集且无法保证改进效果。人类专家无法系统性地检查跨多样真实工作负载的十亿级指令trace，因此需要一种自动化的预取器设计方法。

Method: 提出性能异常驱动的自动研究流程：每次迭代首先定位高影响的"无法解释的缺失"到程序计数器（PC），然后提供给LLM代理硬件日志、源代码和切片trace；代理通过可运行的最小化案例验证诊断，并为重复出现的模式家族合成专门的子预取器；实测性能和剩余异常反馈到后续迭代，实现"模拟器在环"的发现机制，突破模型先验限制。最终将所有子预取器组成MoP（Mixture of Prefetchers）。整个过程消耗了19.1亿个DeepSeek V4 Pro tokens。

Result: 在SPEC CPU2006和SPEC CPU2017上，MoP相较于无预取实现了61.1%的几何平均IPC加速比，超越了人工设计的Alecto（+14.5%）、Berti（+21.6%）和Pythia（+23.6%）。在6nm工艺库下的RTL综合显示仅需110KB片上存储和0.0347mm²面积，具有实际可部署性。

Conclusion: 这是首个实证证明：智能体驱动的硬件设计流程能够产生RTL实际可用、且在未见工作负载上超越最先进人工设计的预取器。该工作展示了LLM驱动的自动硬件设计范式在架构探索中的可行性，为未来硬件自动化设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Hardware prefetchers are crucial to processor performance, yet their design remains labor-intensive and expert-driven. Architects inspect execution and memory-access traces, identify patterns, translate them into online hardware heuristics, and evaluate them in simulation, often with no guarantee of improvement. Human experts cannot systematically inspect billion-instruction traces across diverse real-world workloads.
  We present a performance-anomaly-driven autoresearch flow that repeatedly asks why a deployed prefetcher fails and uses the diagnoses to construct the Mixture of Prefetchers (MoP). Each iteration localizes high-impact unexplained misses to program counters, gives agents hardware logs, source code, and sliced traces, validates diagnoses through runnable minimal cases, and synthesizes specialized sub-prefetchers for recurring pattern families. Measured performance and remaining anomalies feed subsequent iterations, enabling simulator-in-the-loop discovery beyond model priors.
  The campaign consumes 1.91 billion DeepSeek V4 Pro tokens. On SPEC CPU2006 and SPEC CPU2017, MoP achieves a 61.1% geomean IPC speedup over no prefetching, outperforming the human-designed Alecto, Berti, and Pythia prefetchers by 14.5%, 21.6%, and 23.6%, respectively. RTL synthesis in a 6nm library reports 110 KB of on-chip storage and 0.0347 mm^2 area. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration that an agent-driven hardware-design process can produce an RTL-practical prefetcher that outperforms state-of-the-art human designs on unseen workloads.

</details>


### [191] [Potential Applications of HBF in LLM Serving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13127)
*Yihan Yin,Yinlun Zhao,Zhixin Yun,Guanying Wu,Feng Zhu,Kai Tao,Shu Li,Fei Huang,Zhe Zhang,Shuangchen Li,Hongzhong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了高带宽闪存（HBF）作为HBM容量扩展在LLM推理服务中的应用，通过将HBF集成到GPU内存层次结构中，扩展模型权重的驻留集，从而提升MoE和多模型服务的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理服务日益受限于内存容量，因为模型权重、KV缓存和服务的模型变体数量持续增长，HBM容量成为瓶颈。

Method: 论文首先讨论如何将HBF集成到GPU内存层次结构中而不损害计算芯片期望的带宽，然后从系统层面将HBF的价值建模为对只读模型状态对象的驻留扩展，并通过模拟评估其对MoE服务（增加专家副本）和多模型服务（减少模型加载、支持热模型复制）的收益。

Result: 模拟结果表明，HBF带来的收益取决于保持HBM驻留执行路径的同时，利用HBF扩展模型权重的驻留集。

Conclusion: HBF作为容量导向的HBM扩展具有系统级价值，但其前提是维持HBM驻留的核心执行路径，HBF仅用于扩大模型权重的驻留集合。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly constrained by memory capacity as model weights, KV caches, and the number of served model variants continue to grow. This report examines High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) as a capacity-oriented extension to HBM-based serving systems. We first discuss how HBF can be integrated into the GPU memory hierarchy without undermining the bandwidth expected by the compute die. We then model the system-level value of added capacity as expanded residency for read-mostly model-state objects. Under this view, HBF can improve MoE serving by enabling more expert replicas and can improve multi-model serving by reducing model loading and supporting hot-model replication. Our simulation results show that these benefits depend on preserving the HBM-resident execution path while using HBF to expand the resident set of model weights.

</details>


### [192] [ROLoad-PMP: Securing Sensitive Operations for Kernels and Bare-Metal Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13287)
*Wende Tan,Chenyang Li,Yangyu Chen,Yuan Li,Chao Zhang,Jianping Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ROLload-PMP，一种基于RISC-V的轻量级软硬件协同设计解决方案，通过引入新指令只从带特定密钥的只读内存加载数据，并在编译时对敏感操作的操作数进行分类和放置，以保护低层软件（如内核和裸机固件）的敏感操作（如控制流转移）免受劫持攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者常利用攻击者控制的输入劫持敏感操作（如控制流转移）来入侵受害者系统。现有解决方案只能部分保护这些目标且性能开销高，在资源受限的IoT设备或内核/裸机固件等低层软件中难以部署。

Method: ROLload-PMP采用软硬件协同设计：1) 提出新指令，仅从带特定密钥的只读内存区域加载数据，保证（可能被破坏的）数据指针指向对象（pointee）的完整性；2) 提供程序加固机制，在编译时将敏感操作的操作数分类并放置到不同密钥的只读内存中，运行时使用ROLload-PMP系列指令加载。

Result: 基于RISC-V的FPGA原型实现表明，ROLload-PMP仅增加极少硬件资源（<1.40%），支持开销可忽略（<0.853%）的轻量级防御方案，并提供比ARM BTI和Intel CET等现有硬件解决方案更广泛、更强的安全保障。

Conclusion: ROLload-PMP是一种实用且高效的方案，能够在资源受限的低层软件环境中以极低开销实现更强的安全防护，特别是前向边控制流完整性（forward-edge CFI）等关键防御应用，优于现有主流硬件安全方案。

Abstract: A common way for attackers to compromise victim systems is hijacking sensitive operations (e.g., control-flow transfers) with attacker-controlled inputs. Existing solutions in general only protect parts of these targets and have high performance overheads, which are impractical and hard to deploy on systems with limited resources (e.g., IoT devices) or for low-level software like kernels and bare-metal firmware. In this paper, we present a lightweight hardware-software co-design solution ROLoad-PMP to protect sensitive operations from being hijacked for low-level software. First, we propose new instructions, which only load data from read-only memory regions with specific keys, to guarantee the integrity of pointees pointed by (potentially corrupted) data pointers. Then, we provide a program hardening mechanism to protect sensitive operations, by classifying and placing their operands into read-only memory with different keys at compile-time and loading them with ROLoad-PMP-family instructions at runtime. We have implemented an FPGA-based prototype of ROLoad-PMP based on RISC-V, and demonstrated an important defense application, i.e., forward-edge control-flow integrity. Results showed that ROLoad-PMP only costs few extra hardware resources (< 1.40%). Moreover, it enables many lightweight (e.g., with negligible overheads < 0.853%) defenses, and provides broader and stronger security guarantees than existing hardware solutions, e.g., ARM BTI and Intel CET.

</details>


### [193] [YAVIN: A Unified Architecture for Secure Edge Processing in Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13496)
*Shouzhi Fang,William C. Tegge,Md Omar Faruque,Peipei Zhou,Endadul Hoque,Alex K. Jones*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: YAVIN提出了一种统一的可信计算基（TCB），将传统TEE的可信边界从处理器扩展到包含专用内存区域的PIM执行，并首次在PIM中实现LightSaber KEM后量子密码系统和ASCON-128认证加密，实现数据在处理器和内存间的加密计算。相比最新的PIM AES实现提速20倍以上，在INT8/INT32量化边缘LLM上仅引入34%和9.3%的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE仅在处理器内建立信任，内存总线被视为不可信资源，因此无法在内存中直接执行可信计算。随着PIM成为减少冯·诺依曼瓶颈的有效手段，如何在多租户边缘计算环境中同时保障安全性和隐私，并支持在内存中进行可信计算，成为一个关键挑战。

Method: YAVIN利用传统TEE架构中已有的专用受保护内存区域，将可信边界扩展至处理器和PIM执行。它首次在PIM中实现了LightSaber KEM后量子公钥密码系统和ASCON-128认证加密算法，并针对DRAM执行进行算法-硬件协同设计以建立和维持共享密码状态。此外，通过密码学与PIM的协同设计，对张量工作负载重新组织计算以满足认证加密的排序约束，同时采用比特切片排序限制临时明文暴露。

Result: 相比最新的PIM AES实现，YAVIN实现超过20倍的加速；在执行INT8和INT32量化边缘LLM时，相对于明文执行分别仅引入34%和9.3%的开销。

Conclusion: YAVIN证明了构建覆盖处理器与内存的统一可信计算基是可行且高效的，通过密码学-PIM协同设计能够在满足安全约束的同时实现低开销的可信就地计算，为边缘计算中的安全多租户PIM执行提供了新范式。

Abstract: Secure, private multi-tenant execution spanning processors, memory, and accelerators remains one of the most significant challenges in modern edge computing systems. Simultaneously, processing-in-memory (PIM) has emerged as an effective approach for reducing the Von Neumann bottleneck by moving computation closer to data. Existing trusted execution environments (TEEs) establish trust only within the processor, protecting data while it traverses untrusted resources such as the memory bus. Consequently, trusted computation cannot be performed directly within memory. We present YAVIN, a unified trusted computing base (TCB) that extends the TEE beyond the processor to encompass both processor execution and a dedicated memory region supporting trusted processing-in-memory execution while treating the memory bus as untrusted. Leveraging the dedicated protected memory regions already established by conventional TEE architectures, YAVIN enables data to be decrypted, processed, and re-encrypted by either processor or PIM execution while remaining within the TEE. To realize this unified TCB, YAVIN presents the first PIM implementations of the LightSaber KEM post-quantum cryptosystem and ASCON-128 authenticated encryption, co-designing both algorithms for efficient DRAM execution to establish and maintain shared cryptographic state. Finally, we demonstrate how cryptography-PIM co-design for tensor-based workloads reorganizes computation to satisfy the ordering constraints imposed by authenticated encryption with minimal performance overhead while simultaneously enabling bit-sliced ordering that limits temporary plaintext exposure. Compared to the latest PIM AES implementation, YAVIN achieves more than a 20x speedup while incurring only 34% and 9.3% overhead when executing INT8 and INT32 quantized edge-class LLMs, respectively, relative to plaintext execution.

</details>
